Test the simplified form with its users; use modularisation or multiple coordinated views when a single small diagram would conceal essential interactions.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Users cannot reliably interpret a model, or model changes introduce structural errors.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Remove an ambiguity or improve a label before imposing a new modelling standard or decomposition programme.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A known task and user population; explicit behaviour or meaning to preserve; a comparison with the current representation.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e2.md#r-e2a4c4f4cf9253e669813fac2d191cd6ea010b615255f162f241e2a422e83441">Complete shared assumptions · e2a4c4f4cf92</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Modellers propose changes; users and domain experts check that useful meaning has not been lost.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate the relation between simplified and detailed views and the exceptions intentionally retained.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><dl><dt>Decision</dt><dd><p>Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. Users cannot reliably interpret a model, or model changes introduce structural errors.</p></dd><dt>Consumer and authority</dt><dd><p>Modellers propose changes; users and domain experts check that useful meaning has not been lost.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Users handle the relevant cases at least adequately without loss of critical constraints or relationships.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Decomposition hides cross-boundary dependencies; adherence to a size rule increases navigation cost.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Decomposition hides cross-boundary dependencies; adherence to a size rule increases navigation cost.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S010’s small guideline experiment did not establish the tested benefit; S044’s positive notation redesign has one-platform/cohort limits. Simpler-looking is not universally better.</p></dd><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>Source criticism, source-specific validity limits and explicitly identified analytical objections; not every entry is an independent negative outcome study.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Assumption sensitivity</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e2.md#r-e2a4c4f4cf9253e669813fac2d191cd6ea010b615255f162f241e2a422e83441">Complete shared ASSUMPTION SENSITIVITY · e2a4c4f4cf92</a></p></dd><dt>Composition relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>R005</p></li><li><p>R046</p></li><li><p>R050</p></li><li><p>R063</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Duplicate or supersession links</dt><dd><ol><li><dl><dt>Relation</dt><dd><p>QUALIFIES_OR_SUPERSEDES_UNIVERSALISATION</p></dd><dt>Property id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-063</p></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>The rejected proposition remains in the candidate denominator.</p></dd></dl></li></ol></dd><dt>Open questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>When do multiple views reduce cognitive burden rather than fragment understanding?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>The protected comprehension function survives, while formal compliance or complexity thresholds can become counterproductive ceremony. ## EBPM:EBPM-013

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-002.md#EBPM-EBPM-013">EBPM:EBPM-013</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-013</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Interpretable activity labels and domain terms</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B3</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S009</p></li><li><p>S044</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Resolve ambiguous domain terms before standardising a label grammar; test important labels through concrete cases.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Users disagree about an activity, decision or completion label, or labels are reused across roles.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A brief glossary or example can be enough; a mandatory naming convention is unnecessary when existing terms work.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Knowledge of the user vocabulary and the distinctions the model must communicate.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/16.md#r-161e226be4541827538d9fc20c07cb59bcca824aeb03bcac4d0bba03249ad1f4">Complete shared assumptions · 161e226be454</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Domain participants determine meaning; <a id="EBPM-EBPM-016"></a>
## EBPM:EBPM-016

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-002.md#EBPM-EBPM-016">EBPM:EBPM-016</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-016</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Knowledge-work and declarative alternatives</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B3</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S014</p></li><li><p>S012</p></li><li><p>S042</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>CONTEXT_DEPENDENT</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/8d.md#r-8d2a88912a2d04ed9135dc1d751b4f8e1a3538859c99b2aab97f7c811f30f923">Complete shared evidence limit · 8d2a88912a2d</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Knowledge-work support, discretion, variants and binding constraints</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Participants can adapt the work while preserving essential obligations and keeping others informed.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Select support according to what can be known in advance: goals and information needs, mandatory constraints, planned tasks or a stable flow. Allow structured sub-processes inside knowledge work and avoid making flexibility equivalent to the absence of guidance.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Cases require changing knowledge, professional judgement or runtime planning that an exhaustive flow would distort.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A shared case note, goal list and essential constraints can be enough without a case-management engine.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Competent participants; intelligible goals/constraints; usable shared information and a way to recognise unresolved obligations.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b8.md#r-b804be0f3c95ceb70be6c9f820033454f98e06f957c5d0982735fe7cf72b75c2">Complete shared assumptions · b804be0f3c95</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Case workers plan within professional and organisational authority; system flexibility does not grant new powers.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange case knowledge, decisions and changes in responsibility rather than only next-task messages.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/69.md#r-6971c3dda29464d0afafa77b3fe81bf8fddca66cd20c90f6fe7d74d49b6a48c4">Complete shared consumer · 6971c3dda294</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Participants can adapt the work while preserving essential obligations and keeping others informed.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Discretion becomes unaccountable; a constraint model is too difficult to understand; supposedly flexible tools impose hidden rigidity.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Discretion becomes unaccountable; a constraint model is too difficult to understand; supposedly flexible tools impose hidden rigidity.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S014 is conceptual/illustrative and S012 normative. S042’s formal admissibility does not prove suitability for every knowledge worker. The formal checking mechanism is separately registered as EBPM-073.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S012">EBPM:S012</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S014">EBPM:S014</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S042">EBPM:S042</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R006</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R009</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A shared case note, goal list and essential constraints can be enough without a case-management engine.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. <dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-002.md#EBPM-EBPM-018">EBPM:EBPM-018</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-018</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Elapsed time distinguished from work effort</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B4</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S016</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/a9.md#r-a9b23babcefdab50282c806ca25415b16534f1bd72bc7c4c6886a75ca70e9093">Complete shared evidence limit · a9b23babcefd</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Elapsed process time, effort and resource occupancy claims</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A delay claim no longer conflates a worker’s effort with the case’s elapsed duration.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Separate elapsed time experienced by the case or recipient from active work effort and resource occupancy. Locate waiting, transfer and rework contributions before attributing delay to an actor or deciding which activity to accelerate.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A time-based performance claim or delay intervention is being made.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A manually reconstructed case timeline can be adequate; detailed time logging is unnecessary when the causal bottleneck is already clear.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Consistent start/end and clock semantics; relevant case classes; treatment of overlapping work and unlogged waiting.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/80.md#r-806a51468ddd02ffd0d73fc0ef9900dfaf53c079e84fc31fd45490554b632f49">Complete shared assumptions · 806a51468ddd</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analysts interpret time evidence with performers; owners can change allocation or handoff arrangements within their authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Show the timeline and distinguish work, queueing, handoff and interruption interpretations.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/8e.md#r-8e52e2fa4f3e0afb41c41ac97a2def0b3aa237a0bd98282f2110089e0fb3cae0">Complete shared consumer · 8e52e2fa4f3e</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A delay claim no longer conflates a worker’s effort with the case’s elapsed duration.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Timestamps measure data entry, not work; parallel activity is double-counted; monitoring imposes intrusive effort tracking.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Timestamps measure data entry, not work; parallel activity is double-counted; monitoring imposes intrusive effort tracking.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S017 cautions against simplifying human-resource behaviour. The distinction is sound, but accurate component attribution remains an empirical question.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S002">EBPM:S002</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S016">EBPM:S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S017">EBPM:S017</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S027">EBPM:S027</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R011</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R012</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A manually reconstructed case timeline can be adequate; detailed time logging is unnecessary when the causal bottleneck is already clear.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Which component of elapsed time is both consequential and changeable without shifting burden?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/02.md#r-028481050204009140ff6605edfe10324613551c39b9af3094016f40fa8669f7">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 028481050204</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retained as a robust criterion/mechanism distinction within the declared trigger; this is not a claim of universally quantified economic benefit. Use a queueing model or simulation only when its assumptions represent the relevant work; recheck the end-to-end constraint after a local change.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Persistent queues, fluctuating demand or shared-resource contention dominate outcomes.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A workload/timeline comparison and a bounded allocation trial may dominate building a detailed stochastic model.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Meaningful arrival/service observations, resource availability and allocation assumptions; an appropriate steady-state or transient scope.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/25.md#r-2584485f457fcb67965532728e517846458eeb98e4e03bd06a572d69dcd8ca61">Complete shared assumptions · 2584485f457f</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Resource managers can change capacity and assignment; analysts supply conditional predictions, not authority to alter staffing or worker obligations.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Make demand, capacity and behavioural assumptions explicit to those who allocate resources and bear workload.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/3e.md#r-3ea07883b1fede17837c3daed7f01e447aa827dbeea86e770b90d1cc8e5f5de7">Complete shared consumer · 3ea07883b1fe</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The selected intervention targets a supported constraint and includes evidence about the resulting queue and displaced load.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Average-rate assumptions hide bursts; human multitasking violates resource models; a bottleneck merely moves downstream.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Average-rate assumptions hide bursts; human multitasking violates resource models; a bottleneck merely moves downstream.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S017 and S018 show assumption dependence; S027 demonstrates data and organisational complications. removing it must not destroy a real protective or accountability function.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Property kind</dt><dd><p>CRITERION_COUPLED_TO_OPERATING_METHOD</p></dd><dt>Evidence strength partitions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/98.md#r-98f475ae86f92af68020e747825970deb01e05bb44fbdf778a0055a47159aa50">Complete shared EVIDENCE STRENGTH PARTITIONS · 98f475ae86f9</a></p></dd><dt>Primary source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S001</p></li><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S016</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Empirical or case source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S001</p></li><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S016</p></li><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Contrary evidence</dt><dd><dl><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Adjudication</dt><dd><p>S016’s practitioner objections preserve training and segregation constraints;
